#include "virtio_blk.h"
#include "pci.h"
#include "uart.h"
/* Legacy vring: avail immediately follows descriptors, used is page-aligned.
 * Padding avail to a page would VIOLATE the legacy queue layout. */
static uint8_t queue_memory[32768] ALIGN_4K __attribute__((section(".bss.dma")));
static vring_desc *descriptors;
static vring_avail *available;
static vring_used *used;
static uint16_t queue_size;
static struct { uint32_t type,reserved; uint64_t sector; } request ALIGN_64;
static uint8_t dma_page[4096] ALIGN_4K __attribute__((section(".bss.dma")));
static uint8_t status ALIGN_64;
static uint16_t io,last_used;
static bool inflight,failed,write_operation;
static void *read_target;
static uint64_t capacity;
static uint32_t transfer_length;
static bool finish(void) {
 uint64_t start=rdtsc(); bool ok=false;
 while(!virtio_blk_poll(&ok)) {
  if(rdtsc()-start>10000000000ULL) { failed=true; panic("VirtIO timeout: DMA buffers retained"); }
  cpu_pause();
 }
 return ok;
}
void virtio_blk_init(void) {
 pci_device_t d=pci_find_device(0x1af4,0x1001); uint32_t bar,features;
 if(!d.found) {
  d=pci_find_device(0x1af4,0x1042);
  panic(d.found?"modern-only VirtIO unsupported: use transitional device":"VirtIO PCI device absent");
 }
 bar=pci_read(d,0x10);
 if(!(bar&1U)||(bar&~3U)>65500U) panic("VirtIO invalid I/O BAR");
 io=(uint16_t)(bar&~3U); pci_enable_io_dma(d);
 outb(io+18,0); outb(io+18,1); outb(io+18,3);
 features=inl(io);
 if(!(features&(1U<<9))) panic("VirtIO FLUSH required for durability");
 outl(io+4,1U<<9); outw(io+14,0);
 queue_size=inw(io+12);
 uart_puts("VirtIO IO="); uart_put_hex(io); uart_puts(" qsize="); uart_put_dec(queue_size); uart_puts(" PFN="); uart_put_hex(inl(io+8)); uart_puts("\n");
 if((queue_size<128||queue_size>1024||(queue_size&(queue_size-1)))||inl(io+8)!=0) panic("VirtIO queue size unsupported");
 memset(queue_memory,0,sizeof queue_memory);
 descriptors=(vring_desc *)queue_memory;
 available=(vring_avail *)(queue_memory+16U*queue_size);
 used=(vring_used *)(queue_memory+((16U*queue_size+6U+2U*queue_size+4095U)&~4095U));
 outl(io+8,(uint32_t)((uint64_t)queue_memory>>12));
 capacity=(uint64_t)inl(io+20)|((uint64_t)inl(io+24)<<32);
 if(capacity<1024) panic("VirtIO disk too small");
 outb(io+18,7);
 if(inb(io+18)&128) panic("VirtIO device rejected initialization");
 uart_puts("PCI VirtIO legacy detected; DMA queue="); uart_put_dec(queue_size); uart_puts("; FLUSH negotiated\n");
}
static bool submit(uint32_t type,uint64_t sector,const void *page,uint32_t length) {
 vring_desc *d=descriptors; uint16_t a;
 if(!io||failed||inflight||sector>capacity||capacity-sector<length/512) return false;
 if(length>4096||length%512||(!page&&type!=4)) return false;
 transfer_length=length; request.type=type; request.reserved=0; request.sector=sector;
 status=0xff; write_operation=type!=0; read_target=(void *)page;
 if(type==1) memcpy(dma_page,page,length);
 d[0].addr=(uint64_t)&request; d[0].len=16; d[0].flags=1; d[0].next=type==4?2:1;
 d[1].addr=(uint64_t)dma_page; d[1].len=length; d[1].flags=(uint16_t)(1|(type==0?2:0)); d[1].next=2;
 d[2].addr=(uint64_t)&status; d[2].len=1; d[2].flags=2; d[2].next=0;
 a=available->idx; ((uint16_t *)((uint8_t *)available+4))[a%queue_size]=0;
 inflight=true;
 __atomic_store_n(&available->idx,(uint16_t)(a+1),__ATOMIC_RELEASE);
 mfence(); outw(io+16,0); return true;
}
bool virtio_blk_submit(uint64_t sector,const void *page,bool write) { return submit(write?1U:0U,sector,page,4096); }
bool virtio_blk_poll(bool *ok) {
 uint16_t u; uint8_t s;
 if(!inflight) { *ok=false; return true; }
 u=__atomic_load_n(&used->idx,__ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
 if(u==last_used) return false;
 if((uint16_t)(u-last_used)!=1 || ((vring_used_elem *)((uint8_t *)used+4))[last_used%queue_size].id!=0) panic("VirtIO corrupt used ring");
 s=__atomic_load_n(&status,__ATOMIC_ACQUIRE); *ok=s==0;
 if(*ok&&!write_operation) memcpy(read_target,dma_page,transfer_length);
 last_used=u; inflight=false; (void)inb(io+19); return true;
}
bool virtio_blk_write_page(uint64_t sector,const void *page) { return virtio_blk_submit(sector,page,true)&&finish(); }
bool virtio_blk_read_page(uint64_t sector,void *page) { return virtio_blk_submit(sector,page,false)&&finish(); }
bool virtio_blk_flush(void) { return submit(4,0,0,0)&&finish(); }

uint64_t virtio_blk_capacity(void) { return capacity; }
bool virtio_blk_sector(uint64_t sector,void *data,bool write) { return submit(write?1U:0U,sector,data,512)&&finish(); }

ARQUIVO: src/vm.c

#include "admission.h"
/* Total, deterministic stack checker/interpreter, no back edges, memory access
 * or arbitrary calls. ASSERT proves the evaluated integer equality, not Lean. */
bool vm_verify(const uint8_t *code,size_t length) {
 uint64_t stack[16]; uint8_t kind[16]; unsigned sp=0; size_t pc=0; bool assertion=false;
 if(!length||length>256) return false;
 while(pc<length) {
  uint8_t op=code[pc++]; uint64_t a,b;
  switch(op) {
   case 0: return assertion&&sp==0&&pc==length;
   case 1: if(pc==length||sp==16) return false; kind[sp]=0; stack[sp++]=code[pc++]; break;
   case 2: if(sp<2||kind[sp-1]||kind[sp-2]) return false; b=stack[--sp]; a=stack[sp-1]; if(__builtin_add_overflow(a,b,&stack[sp-1])) return false; break;
   case 3: if(sp<2||kind[sp-1]||kind[sp-2]) return false; b=stack[--sp]; stack[sp-1]=(stack[sp-1]==b); kind[sp-1]=1; break;
   case 4: if(!sp||kind[sp-1]!=1||stack[--sp]!=1) return false; assertion=true; break;
   default: return false;
  }
 }
 return false;
}

ARQUIVO: tests/admission_test.py

"""RFC8032 interoperability, negative signatures and type-aware VM corpus."""
import ctypes as C
import pathlib,random,subprocess,re
subprocess.run(['gcc','-shared','-fPIC','-O2','-std=c99','-Wall','-Wextra','-Werror','-pedantic','-Iinclude','-Iinclude/crypto','src/vm.c','src/crypto/monocypher.c','src/crypto/monocypher-ed25519.c','-o','build/admission.so'],check=True)
lib=C.CDLL('./build/admission.so')
verify=lib.crypto_ed25519_check; verify.argtypes=[C.c_char_p,C.c_char_p,C.c_char_p,C.c_size_t]; verify.restype=C.c_int
key=bytes.fromhex('d75a980182b10ab7d54bfed3c964073a0ee172f3daa62325af021a68f707511a')
sig=bytes.fromhex('e5564300c360ac729086e2cc806e828a84877f1eb8e5d974d873e065224901555fb8821590a33bacc61e39701cf9b46bd25bf5f0595bbe24655141438e7a100b')
assert verify(sig,key,b'',0)==0
for i in range(64):
    bad=bytearray(sig); bad[i]^=1; assert verify(bytes(bad),key,b'',0)!=0
assert verify(sig,key,b'x',1)!=0
header=pathlib.Path('build/capsule.h').read_text()
def array(name):
    body=re.search(name+r'\s*\[\s*\]\s*=\s*\{([^}]*)\}',header).group(1)
    return bytes(int(x.strip(),0) for x in body.split(',') if x.strip())
msg=array('capsule_message'); pub=array('authority_public'); signature=array('capsule_signature')
assert verify(signature,pub,msg,len(msg))==0
for i in [0,16,20,len(msg)-1]:
    bad=bytearray(msg); bad[i]^=1; assert verify(signature,pub,bytes(bad),len(bad))!=0
vm=lib.vm_verify; vm.argtypes=[C.c_char_p,C.c_size_t]; vm.restype=C.c_bool
corpus=[(b'\x01\x01\x01\x02\x02\x01\x03\x03\x04\x00',True),
        (b'',False),(b'\x00',False),(b'\x02\x00',False),(b'\x01',False),
        (b'\x01\x01\x04\x00',False), # ASSERT requires boolean, not integer 1
        (b'\x01\x01\x01\x02\x03\x04\x00',False), # false assertion
        (b'\xff',False),(b'\x01\x01'*17+b'\x00',False),
        (b'\x01\x01\x01\x01\x03\x01\x01\x02\x00',False)]
for code,expected in corpus: assert vm(code,len(code))==expected
rng=random.Random(42)
for _ in range(1000):
    a=rng.randrange(128); b=rng.randrange(128)
    code=bytes([1,a,1,b,2,1,a+b,3,4,0]); assert vm(code,len(code))
    bad=code[:-3]+bytes([255]); assert not vm(bad,len(bad))
print('PASS RFC8032 + 69 tamper checks; VM typed negatives + 2000 arithmetic policies')

"""Create a reviewable, deterministic text bundle of all source/build/test files."""
from pathlib import Path
paths = [Path('README.md'), Path('Makefile'), Path('link.ld'), Path('.gitignore')]
paths += sorted(Path('include').rglob('*.h'))
paths += sorted(Path('src').rglob('*.c'))
paths += sorted(Path('tests').glob('*.py'))
paths += sorted(Path('tests').glob('*.c'))
paths += sorted(Path('user').glob('*.c'))
paths += [Path('src/crypto/LICENCE.md'),Path('src/crypto/UPSTREAM.json')]
if Path('build/capsule.h').exists(): paths.append(Path('build/capsule.h'))
parts = ['OMEGA OS — fontes completos da implementação modular\n'
         'Gerado por make bundle. Cada marcador identifica o arquivo original.\n']
for path in paths:
    parts.append(f'\n{"=" * 72}\nARQUIVO: {path.as_posix()}\n{"=" * 72}\n{path.read_text()}')
Path('omega_os_fontes.txt').write_text(''.join(parts))
print(f'omega_os_fontes.txt: {len(paths)} arquivos; {Path("omega_os_fontes.txt").stat().st_size} bytes')

"""Ensure V2 refuses V1 media without silently overwriting old checkpoints."""
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
path=Path('build/v1-preserved.img')
with path.open('wb') as f:
    f.truncate(8*1024*1024)
    f.seek(4096)
    f.write(struct.pack('<Q',0x314c53534147454d))
before=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).digest()
p=subprocess.Popen([sys.argv[1],'-kernel','omega_os.elf','-drive',f'file={path},if=virtio,format=raw',
                    '-smp','2','-m','128M','-serial','stdio','-display','none','-no-reboot'],
                   stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
try:
    out,_=p.communicate(timeout=2)
except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
    p.kill()
    out,_=p.communicate(timeout=5)
assert b'V1 disk needs offline migration; refusing overwrite' in out,out.decode(errors='replace')
assert hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).digest()==before
Path('build/v1-refusal.log').write_bytes(out)
print('PASS V1 format refused; disk image unchanged byte for byte')

ARQUIVO: tests/make_capsule.py

"""Development authority: private key stays on host, never enters the kernel."""
import pathlib,subprocess,argparse
parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(); parser.add_argument('--authority-key',type=pathlib.Path)
args=parser.parse_args()
b=pathlib.Path('build'); key=args.authority_key or b/'dev-authority.pem'
def run(args): subprocess.run(args,check=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
if args.authority_key and not key.is_file(): raise SystemExit('Authority key file not found')
if not key.exists(): run(['openssl','genpkey','-algorithm','ED25519','-out',str(key)])
if not args.authority_key: key.chmod(0o600)
run(['openssl','pkey','-in',str(key),'-pubout','-outform','DER','-out','build/authority.der'])
# Stack VM: PUSH 1, PUSH 2, ADD, PUSH 3, EQ, ASSERT, HALT.
vm=bytes([1,1,1,2,2,1,3,3,4,0])
code=(b/'agent.bin').read_bytes()
assert 0<len(code)<=4096
# Version/domain, fixed code and proof lengths are authenticated as well.
import struct
message=b'OMEGA-CAPSULE-2\0'+struct.pack('<II',len(code),len(vm))+code+vm
(b/'capsule.msg').write_bytes(message)
run(['openssl','pkeyutl','-sign','-rawin','-inkey',str(key),'-in','build/capsule.msg','-out','build/capsule.sig'])
pub=(b/'authority.der').read_bytes()[-32:]
def c_array(name,data): return 'static const unsigned char '+name+'[] = {'+','.join(str(x) for x in data)+'};\n'
(b/'capsule.h').write_text(c_array('authority_public',pub)+c_array('capsule_signature',(b/'capsule.sig').read_bytes())+c_array('capsule_message',message))

========================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/rational_test.py
========================================================================
"""Cross-check freestanding arithmetic against Python exact fractions."""
import ctypes as C
from fractions import Fraction
import random
import subprocess

subprocess.run(['gcc', '-shared', '-fPIC', '-O3', '-Wall', '-Wextra', '-Werror',
                '-std=c99', '-Iinclude', 'src/rational.c', '-o', 'build/rational.so'], check=True)
class R(C.Structure):
    _fields_ = [('num', C.c_longlong), ('den', C.c_longlong)]
lib = C.CDLL('./build/rational.so')
for name in ['add', 'sub', 'mul']:
    f = getattr(lib, 'rational_' + name)
    f.argtypes = [R, R, C.POINTER(R)]
    f.restype = C.c_bool
lib.rational_cmp.argtypes = [R, R, C.POINTER(C.c_int)]
lib.rational_cmp.restype = C.c_bool
rng = random.Random(42)
count = 0
for _ in range(10000):
    a = Fraction(rng.randint(-100000, 100000), rng.randint(1, 100000))
    b = Fraction(rng.randint(-100000, 100000), rng.randint(1, 100000))
    ar, br = R(a.numerator, a.denominator), R(b.numerator, b.denominator)
    for name, expected in [('add', a+b), ('sub', a-b), ('mul', a*b)]:
        out = R()
        assert getattr(lib, 'rational_'+name)(ar, br, C.byref(out))
        assert (out.num, out.den) == (expected.numerator, expected.denominator)
        count += 1
    result = C.c_int()
    assert lib.rational_cmp(ar, br, C.byref(result))
    assert result.value == (a > b) - (a < b)
    count += 1
print(f'PASS: {count} rational operations versus fractions.Fraction')

========================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/v2_validation.py
========================================================================
"""V2 hardware acceptance: atomic roots, timer/SYSCALL processes, Ed25519, P2P DMA."""
from pathlib import Path
import os,selectors,struct,subprocess,sys,time,socket
QEMU=sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv)>1 else 'qemu-system-x86_64'
MASK=(1<<64)-1
MAGIC=0x324f4d454741534c

def crc(data):
    c=0
    for b in data:
        c^=b<<56
        for _ in range(8): c=((c<<1)^(0x42f0e1eba9ea3693 if c>>63 else 0))&MASK
    return c

def roots(image):
    candidates=[]
    with open(image,'rb') as f:
        def block(s,h,limit):
            assert s>=8 and s%8==0 and s+8<=limit
            f.seek(s*512); data=f.read(4096)
            assert len(data)==4096 and crc(data)==h
            return data
        def walk(s,h,depth,prefix,limit,pages):
            words=struct.unpack('<512Q',block(s,h,limit))
            for i in range(256):
                if words[2*i]:
                    if depth==2: pages[(prefix<<8)|i]=(words[2*i],block(words[2*i],words[2*i+1],limit))
                    else: walk(words[2*i],words[2*i+1],depth+1,(prefix<<8)|i,limit,pages)
        for slot in range(2):
            f.seek(slot*512); raw=bytearray(f.read(512))
            if len(raw)<512: continue
            v=struct.unpack('<64Q',raw); raw[40:48]=bytes(8)
            if v[0]!=MAGIC or not v[1] or crc(raw)!=v[5]: continue
            pages={}
            try: walk(v[2],v[3],0,0,v[4],pages)
            except AssertionError: continue
            candidates.append((v[1],pages))
    assert candidates,'no complete global root'
    return sorted(candidates)

def create(path):
    with open(path,'wb') as f: f.truncate(128*1024*1024)

def launch(image,label,crash=0,network=None):
    cmd=[QEMU,'-kernel','omega_os.elf','-smp','2','-m','128M','-serial','stdio','-display','none','-no-reboot','-accel','tcg,thread=multi',
         '-drive',f'file={image},if=virtio,format=raw']
    if crash: cmd+=['-append',f'sls.crash={crash}']
    if network:
        role,port=network
        endpoint=f'listen=127.0.0.1:{port}' if role==1 else f'connect=127.0.0.1:{port}'
        cmd+=['-netdev',f'socket,id=n,{endpoint}','-device',f'virtio-net-pci,netdev=n,mac=52:54:00:12:34:0{role}', '-object',f'filter-dump,id=dump,netdev=n,file=build/{label}.pcap']
    return subprocess.Popen(cmd,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT),label,crash,network

def finish(jobs):
    sel=selectors.DefaultSelector(); buffers={p:bytearray() for p,*_ in jobs}; pending=set(buffers); deadline=time.monotonic()+30
    for p,*_ in jobs: sel.register(p.stdout,selectors.EVENT_READ,p)
    try:
        while pending and time.monotonic()<deadline:
            for key,_ in sel.select(.1):
                p=key.data; chunk=os.read(key.fd,65536)
                if chunk: buffers[p].extend(chunk)
                if b'FAIL:' in buffers[p] or (not chunk and p.poll() is not None): raise AssertionError(buffers[p].decode(errors='replace'))
                job=next(j for j in jobs if j[0] is p)
                marker=f'SLS crash-test boundary={job[2]}\n'.encode() if job[2] else b'complete; HLT\n'
                if marker in buffers[p]: pending.discard(p); sel.unregister(p.stdout)
        assert not pending,'QEMU progress timeout: '+repr([buffers[p].decode(errors='replace') for p in pending])
        texts=[]
        for p,label,crash,network in jobs:
            text=buffers[p].decode(errors='replace'); texts.append(text)
            if not crash:
                for m in ['noncanonical syscall stack quarantined without SYSRET PASS','private peer page PF + non-executable stack PF PASS','SMP SPSC 65536','Ed25519 external signature','private CR3; Ring3 IPC syscalls PASS','Ring3 PF/GP + disk rollback PASS','SLS global atomic epoch=']:
                    assert m in text,(label,m,text)
                if network: assert 'two-node Ethernet + peer durable commit PASS' in text,text
        return texts
    finally:
        for p,label,*_ in jobs:
            if p.poll() is None: p.kill()
            p.wait(timeout=5); Path(f'build/{label}.log').write_bytes(buffers[p])
        sel.close()

def boot(image,label,crash=0): return finish([launch(image,label,crash)])[0]
def epochs(pages): return tuple(struct.unpack_from('<Q',pages[i][1],80)[0] for i in [0,1])
def sparse_copy(source,target):
    subprocess.run(['cp','--sparse=always',str(source),str(target)],check=True)

b=Path('omega_os.elf').read_bytes(); offset=b.find(struct.pack('<I',0x1badb002),0,8192)
assert offset>=0 and offset%4==0 and sum(struct.unpack_from('<3I',b,offset))&0xffffffff==0
image=Path('build/v2-test.img'); create(image)
boot(image,'v2-first'); assert epochs(roots(image)[-1][1])==(65536,65536)
boot(image,'v2-reboot'); assert epochs(roots(image)[-1][1])==(131072,131072)
expected=bytes((j^0xa5)&255 for j in range(4096)); assert roots(image)[-1][1][2][1]==expected
assert roots(image)[-1][1][262143][1][:16]==b'OMEGA-GIB-SPARSE'
for stage in range(1,5):
    cut=Path(f'build/v2-cut-{stage}.img'); sparse_copy(image,cut)
    before=roots(cut)[-1]; boot(cut,f'v2-cut-{stage}',stage); after=roots(cut)[-1]
    assert epochs(after[1]) in [(131072,131072),(131073,131073)],'mixed-page epoch'
    if stage<3: assert after[0]==before[0] and epochs(after[1])==(131072,131072)
    if stage==4: assert after[0]==before[0]+1 and epochs(after[1])==(131073,131073)
    boot(cut,f'v2-cut-{stage}-reboot')
    assert epochs(roots(cut)[-1][1])[0]==epochs(roots(cut)[-1][1])[1]
# A corrupted latest payload invalidates its WHOLE root, not one page only.
cut=Path('build/v2-corrupt.img'); sparse_copy(image,cut)
previous,newest=roots(cut)[-2:]
with cut.open('r+b') as f:
    f.seek(newest[1][262143][0]*512); f.write(b'BROKEN-DATA'); f.flush(); os.fsync(f.fileno())
assert roots(cut)[-1][0]==previous[0]
boot(cut,'v2-corrupt-reboot')
assert epochs(roots(cut)[-1][1])[0]==epochs(roots(cut)[-1][1])[1]
print('PASS atomic multi-page root: four SIGKILL boundaries, two boots, whole-root corruption fallback')
# Two independent bare-metal guests exchange raw Ethernet through QEMU's socket.
with socket.socket() as s: s.bind(('127.0.0.1',0)); port=s.getsockname()[1]
a=Path('build/node-a.img'); c=Path('build/node-b.img'); create(a); create(c)
jobs=[launch(a,'v2-node-a',network=(1,port))]
time.sleep(.15); jobs.append(launch(c,'v2-node-b',network=(2,port)))
finish(jobs)
for path,peer in [(a,2),(c,1)]:
    data=roots(path)[-1][1][3][1]
    assert data[:13]==b'OMEGA-PEER-V2' and struct.unpack_from('<Q',data,16)[0]==0x4f4d454741000000|peer
for node in ['a','b']:
    assert b'OMEGA-USER-DMA' in Path(f'build/v2-node-{node}.pcap').read_bytes(), 'missing direct user TX frame'
boot(a,'v2-peer-state-reboot')
assert roots(a)[-1][1][3][1][:13]==b'OMEGA-PEER-V2'
print('PASS two-node VirtIO-Net RX/TX DMA and peer-state global disk commit')
print('PASS LAPIC preemption, private CR3, SYSCALL/SYSRET IPC, Ed25519, typed bytecode verifier')

========================================================================
ARQUIVO: tests/ipc_stress.c
========================================================================
/* Hosted test only: kernel objects themselves have no libc dependencies. */
#include <pthread.h>
#include <sched.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include "ipc.h"
#define MESSAGES 1000000ULL
static omega_ring_t ring;
static void *produce(void *unused) {
 uint64_t i; (void)unused;
 for(i=0;i<MESSAGES;i++) while(!ring_push(&ring,i)) sched_yield();
 return 0;
}
static void *consume(void *unused) {
 uint64_t i,v; (void)unused;
 for(i=0;i<MESSAGES;i++) {
  while(!ring_pop(&ring,&v)) sched_yield();
  if(v!=i) { fputs("FIFO corruption\n",stderr); exit(1); }
 }
 return 0;
}
int main(void) {
 pthread_t producer,consumer;
 ring_init(&ring);
 if(pthread_create(&producer,0,produce,0)||pthread_create(&consumer,0,consume,0)) return 1;
 if(pthread_join(producer,0)||pthread_join(consumer,0)) return 1;
 puts("PASS: 1000000 SPSC messages between two host threads"); return 0;
}

========================================================================
ARQUIVO: user/agent.c
========================================================================
/* Native Ring 3 program. Compiled separately; no kernel symbols or headers. */
typedef unsigned long long u64;
static u64 ticks(void) { unsigned l,h; __asm__ volatile("lfence; rdtsc":"=a"(l),"=d"(h)::"memory"); return ((u64)h<<32)|l; }
static u64 call(u64 nr,u64 arg) {
 u64 result;
 __asm__ volatile("syscall":"=a"(result):"a"(nr),"D"(arg):"rcx","r11","memory"); return result;
}
void _start(void) {
 volatile u64 *data=(volatile u64 *)0x80002000ULL;
 u64 role=data[0],i;
 if(call(0,0)!=role+1) (void)call(3,1);
 if(role==0) {
  { u64 mac=call(7,0);
   if(mac) { volatile unsigned char *packet=(volatile unsigned char *)0x80003000ULL;
    for(i=0;i<64;i++) packet[i]=0;
    for(i=0;i<6;i++) { packet[i]=255; packet[6+i]=(unsigned char)(mac>>(i*8)); }
    packet[12]=0x88; packet[13]=0xb5;
    { const char text[]="OMEGA-USER-DMA"; for(i=0;i<sizeof(text);i++) packet[14+i]=(unsigned char)text[i]; }
    if(call(4,64)) (void)call(3,1);
   }
  }
  for(i=0;i<256;i++) while(call(1,i)!=0) { __asm__ volatile("pause"); }
  for(;;) { data[1]++; __asm__ volatile("pause"); }
 }
 if(role==1) {
  for(i=0;i<256;i++) {
   u64 value; do { value=call(2,0); } while(value==~0ULL);
   if(value!=i) (void)call(3,1);
  }
  { u64 begin=ticks(); for(i=0;i<1024;i++) (void)call(0,0); data[2]=(ticks()-begin)/1024; }
  data[1]=256; (void)call(3,0);
 }
 if(role==4) { __asm__ volatile("movabsq $0xffff800000000000,%%rsp; xorq %%rax,%%rax; syscall; ud2":::"rax","rcx","r11","memory"); }
 if(role==3) { ((void (*)(void))0x80001000ULL)(); (void)call(3,1); }
 if(role==2) { data[1]=*(volatile u64 *)0x90000000ULL; (void)call(3,1); }
 for(;;) __asm__ volatile("pause");
}
